NOT for open-ended root-cause diagnosis — escalate those to a more capable model. ([refactory-lang/sittir](https://github.com/refactory-lang/sittir/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sittir-codegen.agent.md) · ★1)
- **sittir-research**: Root-cause diagnosis for sittir tree-sitter codegen / render / read-render-parse failures. Use to find WHERE a render or AST-match break originates (wrap vs node vs render) and WHICH codegen source is responsible — before any fix. Read-only: it diagnoses and reports a precise fix location; it does NOT edit code. Knows the diagnostic tools (probe-kind, dump-ast-mismatches, diff-failures), the deprecated-vs-active render path, and the wrap→node→render layering. Pair with sittir-codegen (which implements the fix it pinpoints). ([refactory-lang/sittir](https://github.com/refactory-lang/sittir/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sittir-research.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sittir-review**: Read-only reviewer for sittir codegen changes — audits a diff/PR for DRY (the core correctness rule), the design principles, spec/plan conformance, gate completeness, and generated-output hygiene. Reports prioritized findings + a verdict (ship / fix-then-ship / needs-rework); does NOT edit or regenerate. Completes the triad with sittir-research (diagnosis) + sittir-codegen (implementation). Use after a codegen PR/change is ready for review; the dispatcher names the base ref, scope, and governing spec/plan. ([refactory-lang/sittir](https://github.com/refactory-lang/sittir/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sittir-review.agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
